8344	     "https://www.iana.org/assignments/nipc-problem-types#event-not-\
8345	                                                           registered" /
8346	     "https://www.iana.org/assignments/nipc-problem-types#trigger-\
8347	                                                      already-enabled" /
8348	     "https://www.iana.org/assignments/nipc-problem-types#protocolmap-\
8349	                                                ble-already-connected" /
8350	     "https://www.iana.org/assignments/nipc-problem-types#protocolmap-\
8351	                                                    ble-no-connection" /
8352	     "https://www.iana.org/assignments/nipc-problem-types#protocolmap-\
8353	                                               ble-connection-timeout" /
8354	     "https://www.iana.org/assignments/nipc-problem-types#protocolmap-\
8355	                                                   ble-bonding-failed" /
8356	     "https://www.iana.org/assignments/nipc-problem-types#protocolmap-\
8357	                                                ble-connection-failed" /
8358	     "https://www.iana.org/assignments/nipc-problem-types#protocolmap-\
8359	                                         ble-service-discovery-failed" /
8360	     "https://www.iana.org/assignments/nipc-problem-types#protocolmap-\
8361	                                ble-invalid-service-or-characteristic" /
8362	     "https://www.iana.org/assignments/nipc-problem-types#protocolmap-\
8363	                                            zigbee-connection-timeout" /
8364	     "https://www.iana.org/assignments/nipc-problem-types#protocolmap-\
8365	                                   zigbee-invalid-endpoint-or-cluster" /
8366	     "https://www.iana.org/assignments/nipc-problem-types#extension-\
8367	                                                transmit-invalid-data" /
8368	     "https://www.iana.org/assignments/nipc-problem-types#extension-\
8369	                                                    firmware-rollback" /
8370	     "https://www.iana.org/assignments/nipc-problem-types#extension-\
8371	                                               firmware-update-failed" /
8372	     "about:blank"
8373	   )
8374	   ; ============================================
8375	   ; From: cddl/api/group_event_status_response_array.cddl
8376	   ; ============================================
8377	   GroupEventStatusResponseArray = [* GroupEventStatusResponse]

8379	   GroupEventSuccessResponse = { event: text, deviceId: text }

8381	   ; Each item is either an event+deviceId success or a \
8382	                                                    GroupFailureResponse
8383	   GroupEventStatusResponse = (GroupEventSuccessResponse // \
8384	                                                   GroupFailureResponse)

8386	   GroupFailureResponse = {
8387	     FailureResponse,
8388	     ? deviceId: text
8389	   }

8391	   ; ============================================
8392	   ; From: cddl/api/group_trigger_status_array.cddl
8393	   ; ============================================
8394	   ; Group Trigger status response array and item shape

8396	   GroupTriggerStatusResponseArray = [* GroupTriggerResponse]

8398	   GroupTriggerResponse = (GroupTriggerSuccessResponse // \
8399	                                            GroupTriggerFailureResponse)

8401	   GroupTriggerSuccessResponse = {
8402	     eventName: text,      ; SDF event name
8403	     action: text,      ; NIPC action operation to execute
8404	     deviceId: text     ; UUID (as text)
8405	   }

8407	   GroupTriggerFailureResponse = {
8408	     FailureResponse,
8409	     ? deviceId: text
8410	   }

8412	   ; ============================================
8413	   ; From: cddl/api/trigger_status_array.cddl
8414	   ; ============================================
8415	   ; Trigger status response array and item shape

8417	   TriggerStatusResponseArray = [* TriggerResponse]

8419	   TriggerResponse = {
8420	     eventName: text,      ; SDF event name
8421	     action: text,      ; NIPC action operation to execute
8422	     instanceId: text     ; UUID (as text)
8423	   }

8425	   ; ============================================
8426	   ; From: cddl/api/property_value_array.cddl
8427	   ; ============================================
8428	   PropertyValueArray = [* PropertyValue]

8430	   ; Minimal PropertyValue shape (matches allOf of Property + Value)
8431	   PropertyValue = {
8432	     property: text,        ; SDF global name of the property
8433	     value: b64text         ; base64-encoded bytes (RFC 4648 Section 4)
8434	   }

8436	   ; Helper type for base64-with-padding encoded text
8437	   b64text = text
8438	   ; ============================================
8439	   ; From: cddl/api/property_value_read_response_array.cddl
8440	   ; ============================================
8441	   PropertyValueReadResponseArray = [* \
8442	                                     PropertyValueReadResponseArrayItem]

8444	   PropertyValueReadResponseArrayItem = ( PropertyValue // \
8445	                                                       FailureResponse )

8447	   ; ============================================
8448	   ; From: cddl/api/property_value_response_array.cddl
8449	   ; ============================================
8450	   PropertyValueResponseArray = [* PropertyValueResponseArrayItem]

8452	   PropertyValueResponseArrayItem = ( SuccessResponse // \
8453	                                                       FailureResponse )

8455	   ; Minimal success shape (may be extended)
8456	   SuccessResponse = {
8457	     status: uint
8458	   }

8460	   ; ============================================
8461	   ; From: cddl/api/sdf_reference.cddl
8462	   ; ============================================
8463	   SdfGlobalName = text    ; absolute URI with fragment referencing an \
8464	                                                   sdfThing or sdfObject

8466	   SdfReference = {
8467	     sdfName: SdfGlobalName
8468	   }

8470	   SdfReferenceArray = [* SdfReference]
8471	   ; ============================================
8472	   ; From: cddl/api/connection.cddl
8473	   ; ============================================
8474	   ConnectionRequest = {
8475	     ? retries: uint,
8476	     ? protocol-info-service-map
8477	   }

8479	   ConnectionResponse = {
8480	     id: text,  ; Device ID
8481	     ? protocol-info-service-map
8482	   }

8484	   ; ============================================
8485	   ; From: cddl/api/protocolinfo.cddl
8486	   ; ============================================
8487	   ; Top-level wrappers
8488	   protocol-info-service-map = (
8489	     ? protocolInformation: ble-service-map / zigbee-service-map
8490	   )

8492	   protocol-info-broadcast = (
8493	     ? protocolInformation: ble-broadcast / zigbee-broadcast
8494	   )

8496	   ; BLE protocol information
8497	   ble-service-map = {
8498	     ble: {
8499	       ? services: [* ble-service],
8500	       ? cached: bool,
8501	       ? cacheExpiryDuration: int,
8502	       ? autoUpdate: bool,
8503	       ? bonding: bonding-type,
8504	     }
8505	   }

8507	   bonding-type = "default" / "none" / "justworks" / "passkey" / "oob"

8509	   ble-service = {
8510	     serviceID: uuid,
8511	     ? characteristics: [* ble-characteristic],
8512	   }

8514	   ble-characteristic = {
8515	     characteristicID: uuid,
8516	     ? flags: [* ble-flag],
8517	     ? descriptors: [* ble-descriptor],
8518	   }

8520	   ble-flag = "read" / "write" / "notify" / "indicate" / "write-no-\
8521	                                                               response"

8523	   ble-descriptor = {
8524	     descriptorID: uuid,
8525	   }

8527	   ble-broadcast = {
8528	     ble: {
8529	       ? connectable: bool,
8530	     },
8531	   }

8533	   ; Zigbee protocol information
8534	   zigbee-service-map = {
8535	     zigbee: {
8536	       ? endpoints: [* zigbee-endpoint],
8537	     },
8538	   }

8540	   zigbee-endpoint = {
8541	     endpointID: uint,
8542	     ? clusters: [* zigbee-cluster],
8543	   }

8545	   zigbee-cluster = {
8546	     clusterID: uint,
8547	     ? attributes: [* zigbee-attribute],
8548	   }

8550	   zigbee-attribute = {
8551	     attributeID: uint,
8552	     attributeType: uint,
8553	     ? flags: [* zigbee-flag],
8554	   }

8556	   zigbee-flag = "read" / "write" / "report"

8558	   zigbee-broadcast = {
8559	     zigbee: {
8560	     },
8561	   }

8563	   ; Basic types
8564	   uuid = tstr .regexp "(?i)^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-\
8565	                                                    f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$"
8566	   <CODE ENDS>
